H = suffix_features.shape[1]\\\\n    ts = min(timestep, H - 1)\\\\n\\\\n    with torch.enable_grad():\\\\n        suffix_cur = suffix_features.detach().clone().float().requires_grad_(True)\\\\n \\\"tree\\\": tree}\\n\\n\\ndef query_gt_sdf(position, sdf_gt_data, k=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Query GT SDF at position using IDW interpolation of K nearest neighbors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    dists, idxs = sdf_gt_data[\\\"tree\\\"].query(position, k=k)\\n    if k == 1:\\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\\\"sdf_values\\\"][idxs])\\n    if dists[0] < 1e-10:\\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\\\"sdf_values\\\"][idxs[0]])\\n    weights = 1.0 / dists\\n    weights /= weights.sum()\\n    return float(np.dot(weights, sdf_gt_data[\\\"sdf_values\\\"][idxs]))\\n\\n\\ndef _refine_action_oracle(action, obs, sdf_gt_data, alpha=0.1, delta=0.001,\\n                           refine_margin=0.01, force_refine=False, adaptive_alpha=False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"True oracle SDF sdf_gt_data) -\\n                        query_gt_sdf(pos_minus, sdf_gt_data)) / (2 * delta)\\n\\n        grad_norm = np.linalg.norm(grad)\\n        if grad_norm > 1e-8:\\n            grad_dir = grad / grad_norm\\n            action_refined = action.copy()\\n            action_norm = np.linalg.norm(action[:3])\\n            if adaptive_alpha and action_norm > 1e-6:\\n                alpha_use = \\\"def main():\\\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\\\\\")\\\\n    args = parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    from openpi_client import args.port)\\\\n\\\\n    from libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\\\\n    benchmark_cls = get_benchmark(args.suite)\\\\n    benchmark = benchmark_cls(task_order_index=0, Overall metrics\\\\n    total_episodes = sum(r[\\\\\\\"n_episodes\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results)\\\\n    total_success = sum(r[\\\\\\\"n_success\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results)\\\\n    total_collision_free = sum(r[\\\\\\\"n_collision_free\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results)\\\\n    total_frozen = sum(r[\\\\\\\"n_frozen\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results)\\\\n    overall_tsr = total_success / total_episodes\\\\n    overall_car = total_collision_free / total_episodes\\\\n    overall_frozen_rate = total_frozen / total_episodes\\\\n    overall_avg_disp = np.mean([r[\\\\\\\"avg_max_displacement\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results])\\\\n    total_real_safe = sum(r[\\\\\\\"real_safe_candidates\\\\\\\"] for r in all_results)\\\\n\\\\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Piajrn8bQsma728ws7Snnr","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"import dataclasses\\\\nimport enum\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport socket\\\\n\\\\nimport tyro\\\\n\\\\nfrom openpi.policies import policy as _policy\\\\nfrom openpi.policies import policy_config as _policy_config\\\\nfrom openpi.serving import websocket_policy_server\\\\nfrom openpi.training import config as _config\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass EnvMode(enum.Enum):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Supported environments.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    ALOHA = \\\\\\\"aloha\\\\\\\"\\\\n    ALOHA_SIM = \\\\\\\"aloha_sim\\\\\\\"\\\\n    DROID = \\\\\\\"droid\\\\\\\"\\\\n    LIBERO = \\\\\\\"libero\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\\\nclass Checkpoint:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load a policy from a trained checkpoint.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    # Training config name (e.g., \\\\\\\"pi0_aloha_sim\\\\\\\").\\\\n    config: str\\\\n    # Checkpoint directory (e.g., \\\\\\\"checkpoints/pi0_aloha_sim/exp/10000\\\\\\\").\\\\n    dir: str\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\\\nclass Default:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Use the default policy for the given environment.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\\\nclass Args:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Arguments for the serve_policy script.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    # Environment to serve the policy for. This is only used when serving default policies.\\\\n    env: EnvMode = EnvMode.ALOHA_SIM\\\\n\\\\n    # If provided, will be used in case the \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\" key is not present in the data, or if the model doesn't have a default\\\\n    # prompt.\\\\n    default_prompt: str | None = None\\\\n\\\\n    # Port to serve the policy on.\\\\n    port: int = 8000\\\\n    # Record the policy's behavior for debugging.\\\\n    record: bool = False\\\\n\\\\n    # Specifies how to load the policy. If not provided, the default policy for the environment will be used.\\\\n    policy: Checkpoint | Default = dataclasses.field(default_factory=Default)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# Default checkpoints that should be used for each environment.\\\\nDEFAULT_CHECKPOINT: dict[EnvMode, Checkpoint] = {\\\\n    EnvMode.ALOHA: Checkpoint(\\\\n        config=\\\\\\\"pi05_aloha\\\\\\\",\\\\n        dir=\\\\\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi05_base\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ),\\\\n    EnvMode.ALOHA_SIM: Checkpoint(\\\\n        config=\\\\\\\"pi0_aloha_sim\\\\\\\",\\\\n        dir=\\\\\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi0_aloha_sim\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ),\\\\n    EnvMode.DROID: Checkpoint(\\\\n        config=\\\\\\\"pi05_droid\\\\\\\",\\\\n        dir=\\\\\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi05_droid\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ),\\\\n    EnvMode.LIBERO: Checkpoint(\\\\n        config=\\\\\\\"pi05_libero\\\\\\\",\\\\n        dir=\\\\\\\"./checkpoints/pi05_libero\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ),\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef create_default_policy(env: EnvMode, *, default_prompt: str | None = None) -> _policy.Policy:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Create a default policy for the given environment.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if checkpoint := DEFAULT_CHECKPOINT.get(env):\\\\n        return _policy_config.create_trained_policy(\\\\n            _config.get_config(checkpoint.config), checkpoint.dir, default_prompt=default_prompt\\\\n        )\\\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Unsupported environment mode: {env}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef create_policy(args: Args) -> _policy.Policy:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Create a policy from the given arguments.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    match args.policy:\\\\n        case Checkpoint():\\\\n            return _policy_config.create_trained_policy(\\\\n                _config.get_config(args.policy.config), args.policy.dir, tyro\\n\\nfrom openpi.policies import policy as _policy\\nfrom openpi.policies import policy_config as _policy_config\\nfrom openpi.serving import websocket_policy_server\\nfrom openpi.training import config as _config\\n\\n\\nclass EnvMode(enum.Enum):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Supported environments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    ALOHA = \\\"aloha\\\"\\n    ALOHA_SIM = \\\"aloha_sim\\\"\\n    DROID = \\\"droid\\\"\\n    LIBERO = \\\"libero\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\nclass Checkpoint:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load a policy from a trained checkpoint.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Training config name (e.g., \\\"pi0_aloha_sim\\\").\\n    config: str\\n    # Checkpoint directory (e.g., \\\"checkpoints/pi0_aloha_sim/exp/10000\\\").\\n    dir: str\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\nclass Default:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Use the default policy for the given environment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass\\nclass Args:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Arguments for the serve_policy script.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Environment to serve the policy for. This is only used when serving default policies.\\n    env: EnvMode = EnvMode.ALOHA_SIM\\n\\n    # If provided, will be used in case the \\\"prompt\\\" key is not present in the data, or if the model doesn't have a default\\n    # prompt.\\n    default_prompt: str | None = None\\n\\n    # Port to serve the policy on.\\n    port: int = 8000\\n    # Record the policy's behavior for debugging.\\n    record: bool = False\\n\\n    # Specifies how to load the policy. If not provided, the default policy for the environment will be used.\\n    policy: Checkpoint | Default = dataclasses.field(default_factory=Default)\\n\\n\\n# Default checkpoints that should be used for each environment.\\nDEFAULT_CHECKPOINT: dict[EnvMode, Checkpoint] = {\\n    EnvMode.ALOHA: Checkpoint(\\n        config=\\\"pi05_aloha\\\",\\n        dir=\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi05_base\\\",\\n    ),\\n    EnvMode.ALOHA_SIM: Checkpoint(\\n        config=\\\"pi0_aloha_sim\\\",\\n        dir=\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi0_aloha_sim\\\",\\n    ),\\n    EnvMode.DROID: Checkpoint(\\n        config=\\\"pi05_droid\\\",\\n        dir=\\\"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi05_droid\\\",\\n    ),\\n    EnvMode.LIBERO: Checkpoint(\\n        config=\\\"pi05_libero\\\",\\n        dir=\\\"./checkpoints/pi05_libero\\\",\\n    ),\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef create_default_policy(env: EnvMode, *, default_prompt: str | None = None) -> _policy.Policy:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Create a default policy for the given environment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if checkpoint := DEFAULT_CHECKPOINT.get(env):\\n        return _policy_config.create_trained_policy(\\n            _config.get_config(checkpoint.config), checkpoint.dir, default_prompt=default_prompt\\n        )\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\"Unsupported environment mode: {env}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef create_policy(args: Args) -> _policy.Policy:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Create a policy from the given arguments.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    match args.policy:\\n        case Checkpoint():\\n            return _policy_config.create_trained_policy(\\n                _config.get_config(args.policy.config), args.policy.dir, default_prompt=args.default_prompt\\n            )\\n        case Default():\\n            return create_default_policy(args.env, default_prompt=args.default_prompt)\\n\\n\\ndef main(args: Args) -> None:\\n    policy = create_policy(args)\\n    policy_metadata = policy.metadata\\n\\n    # Record the policy's behavior.\\n    if args.record:\\n        policy = _policy.PolicyRecorder(policy, \\\"policy_records\\\")\\n\\n    hostname = socket.gethostname()\\n    local_ip = socket.gethostbyname(hostname)\\n    logging.info(\\\"Creating server (host: %s, ip: %s)\\\", hostname, local_ip)\\n\\n    server = websocket_policy_server.WebsocketPolicyServer(\\n        policy=policy,\\n        host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\",\\n        port=args.port,\\n        metadata=policy_metadata,\\n    )\\n    server.serve_forever()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True)\\n    main(tyro.cli(Args))\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}

\\\\\\\"yellow_book_obstacle_1\\\\\\\",\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nSUITE_OBSTACLE_NAMES = {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"safelibero_spatial\\\\\\\": OBSTACLE_NAMES_DEFAULT,\\\\n    \\\\\\\"safelibero_object\\\\\\\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nEvaluate Pi0.5 on SafeLIBERO benchmark via websocket client.\\nConnects to an already-running serve_policy.py server.\\nMeasures CAR, TSR, and per-episode obstacle displacement.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport collections\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport pathlib\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\n\\nimport numpy as torch\\n_orig_torch_load = torch.load\\ndef _safe_torch_load(f, *a, **kw):\\n    kw.setdefault(\\\"weights_only\\\", False)\\n    return _orig_torch_load(f, *a, **kw)\\ntorch.load = _safe_torch_load\\n\\n# Override libero submodule path to use SafeLIBERO benchmark\\nimport libero, libero.libero\\nlibero.libero.__path__.insert(0, os.path.join(SAFELIBERO_PATH, \\\"libero\\\", \\\"libero\\\"))\\n\\nCOLLISION_THRESHOLD = 0.001\\nLIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION = 256\\n\\nOBSTACLE_NAMES_DEFAULT = [\\n    \\\"moka_pot_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"white_storage_box_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"milk_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"wine_bottle_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"red_coffee_mug_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"yellow_book_obstacle_1\\\",\\n]\\n\\nOBSTACLE_NAMES_LONG = [\\n    \\\"moka_pot_small_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"white_storage_box_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"milk_small_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"wine_bottle_small_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"red_coffee_mug_obstacle_1\\\",\\n    \\\"yellow_book_obstacle_1\\\",\\n]\\n\\nSUITE_OBSTACLE_NAMES = {\\n    \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\": OBSTACLE_NAMES_DEFAULT,\\n    \\\"safelibero_object\\\": OBSTACLE_NAMES_DEFAULT,\\n    \\\"safelibero_goal\\\": OBSTACLE_NAMES_DEFAULT,\\n    \\\"safelibero_long\\\": OBSTACLE_NAMES_LONG,\\n}\\n\\nLIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION = [0.0] * 6 + [-1.0]\\n\\n\\ndef get_obstacle_positions(sim, obstacle_names):\\n    positions = {}\\n    for name in obstacle_names:\\n        try:\\n            body_id = sim.model.body_name2id(name + \\\"_main\\\")\\n            pos = sim.data.body_xpos[body_id].copy()\\n            positions[name] = pos\\n        except Exception:\\n            try:\\n                joint_name = name + \\\"_joint0\\\"\\n                joint_id = sim.model.joint_name2id(joint_name)\\n                qpos_addr = sim.model.jnt_qposadr[joint_id]\\n                pos = sim.data.qpos[qpos_addr:qpos_addr + 3].copy()\\n                positions[name] = pos\\n            except Exception:\\n                pass\\n    return positions\\n\\n\\ndef check_collision(initial_positions, current_positions, threshold=COLLISION_THRESHOLD):\\n    for name in initial_positions:\\n        if name in current_positions:\\n            displacement = np.sum(np.abs(\\n                current_positions[name] - initial_positions[name]\\n            ))\\n            if displacement > threshold:\\n                return True, name, displacement\\n    return False, None, 0.0\\n\\n\\ndef quat2axisangle(quat):\\n    if quat[3] > 1.0:\\n        quat[3] = 1.0\\n    elif quat[3] < -1.0:\\n        quat[3] = -1.0\\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - quat[3] * quat[3])\\n    if math.isclose(den, 0.0):\\n        return np.zeros(3)\\n    return (quat[:3] * 2.0 * math.acos(quat[3])) / den\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, task_id):\\n    from libero.libero import get_libero_path\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\n    from openpi_client import image_tools\\n\\n    task = benchmark.get_task(task_id)\\n    bddl_path = benchmark.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_id)\\n    language = task.language\\n\\n    logging.info(\\\"Task %d: %s\\\", task_id, language)\\n    logging.info(\\\"BDDL: %s\\\", bddl_path)\\n\\n    env_args = {\\n        \\\"bddl_file_name\\\": bddl_path,\\n        \\\"camera_heights\\\": LIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION,\\n        \\\"camera_widths\\\": LIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION,\\n    }\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n    env.seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    init_states = benchmark.get_task_init_states(task_id)\\n    n_init_states = init_states.shape[0]\\n\\n    results = []\\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    for ep in range(args.n_episodes):\\n        init_idx = ep % n_init_states\\n        init_state = init_states[init_idx]\\n\\n        env.reset()\\n        obs = env.set\\n\\n... Preprocess images: rotate 180 to match pi0.5 training data\\n            img = np.ascontiguousarray(obs[\\\"agentview_image\\\"][::-1, ::-1])\\n            wrist_img = np.ascontiguousarray(obs[\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\"][::-1, ::-1])\\n\\n            img = image_tools.convert_to_uint8(\\n                image_tools.resize_with_pad(img, args.resize_size, args.resize_size)\\n            )\\n            wrist_img = image_tools.convert_to_uint8(\\n                image_tools.resize_with_pad(wrist_img, args.resize_size, args.resize_size)\\n            )\\n\\n            if not action_plan:\\n                element = {\\n                    \\\"observation/image\\\": img,\\n                    \\\"observation/wrist_image\\\": wrist_img,\\n                    \\\"observation/state\\\": np.concatenate((\\n                        obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"],\\n                        quat2axisangle(obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"].copy()),\\n                        obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"],\\n                    )),\\n                    \\\"prompt\\\": str(language),\\n                }\\n                action_chunk = client.infer(element)[\\\"actions\\\"]\\n                assert len(action_chunk) >= args.replan_steps\\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:args.replan_steps])\\n\\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\\n            action_arr = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\\n            action_norms.append(np.linalg.norm(action_arr))\\n            action_vectors.append(action_arr)\\n\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\\n\\n            # Track EE displacement\\n            curr_ee_pos = obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"]\\n            robot_total_displacement += np.linalg.norm(curr_ee_pos - prev_ee_pos)\\n            prev_ee_pos = curr_ee_pos.copy()\\n\\n            # Track per-obstacle displacement and EE distance\\n            current_positions = get_obstacle_positions(env.sim, obstacle_names)\\n            for name in initial_positions:\\n                if name in current_positions:\\n                    d = np.sum(np.abs(current_positions[name] - initial_positions[name]))\\n                    max_displacement = max(max_displacement, d)\\n                    per_obstacle_max_disp[name] = max(per_obstacle_max_disp[name], d)\\n                    ee_obs_dist = np.linalg.norm(curr_ee_pos - current_positions[name])\\n                    min_ee_obstacle_distance = min(min_ee_obstacle_distance, ee_obs_dist)\\n                    per_obstacle_min_ee_dist[name] = min(per_obstacle_min_ee_dist[name], ee_obs_dist)\\n                    if d > args.collision_threshold and not any(c[\\\"name\\\"] == name for c in all_collided_obstacles):\\n                        all_collided_obstacles.append({\\\"name\\\": name, \\\"step\\\": step, \\\"displacement\\\": float(d)})\\n                        if not collided:\\n                            collided = True\\n                            collision_step = step\\n                            collision_obstacle = name\\n\\n            if done:\\n                success = True\\n                break\\n\\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n        is_frozen = robot_total_displacement < 0.001\\n        if min_ee_obstacle_distance == float('inf'):\\n            min_ee_obstacle_distance = -1.0\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01JdRuEBJAfiBWYJq5WMpBR1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XhQDQVwNpRrPBskTgnBTE8","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-35807","command":"cat > /tmp/patch_pi05.py << 'PATCHEOF'\nimport sys\n\nfilepath = '/root/colliforce-vla/scripts/eval_pi05_safelibero.py'\n\nwith open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\noriginal = content\n\n# 1. Update docstring\ncontent = content.replace(\n    '\"\"\"\\nEvaluate Pi0.5 on SafeLIBERO benchmark via websocket client.\\nConnects to an already-running serve_policy.py server.\\nMeasures CAR, TSR, and per-episode obstacle displacement.\\n\"\"\"',\n    '\"\"\"\\nEvaluate Pi0.5 on SafeLIBERO benchmark via websocket client.\\nConnects to an already-running serve_policy.py server.\\nMeasures CAR, TSR, and per-episode obstacle displacement.\\n\\nOracle SDF refinement functions ported from eval_safelibero.py for Claim 2 validation.\\n\"\"\"',\n    1\n)\n\n# 2. Add cKDTree import\ncontent = content.replace(\n    'import numpy as np\\n',\n    'import numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.spatial import cKDTree\\n',\n    1\n)\n\n# 3. Add oracle functions before evaluate_task + modify signature\noracle_funcs = '''\n\n# --- Oracle SDF refinement (ported from eval_safelibero.py) ---\n\ndef _load_sdf_gt_oracle(path):\n    \"\"\"Load GT SDF data for true oracle refinement.\"\"\"\n    data = np.load(path)\n    points = data[\"query_points\"].astype(np.float64)\n    sdf_values = data[\"sdf_values\"].astype(np.float64)\n    tree = cKDTree(points)\n    logging.info(\"Loaded GT SDF oracle: %d points from %s\", len(points), path)\n    return {\"points\": points, \"sdf_values\": sdf_values, \"tree\": tree}\n\n\ndef query_gt_sdf(position, sdf_gt_data, k=8):\n    \"\"\"Query GT SDF at position using IDW interpolation.\"\"\"\n    dists, idxs = sdf_gt_data[\"tree\"].query(position, k=k)\n    if k == 1:\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs])\n    if dists[0] < 1e-10:\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs[0]])\n    weights = 1.0 / dists\n    weights /= weights.sum()\n    return float(np.dot(weights, sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs]))\n\n\ndef _refine_action_oracle(action, obs, sdf_gt_data, alpha=0.1, delta=0.001,\n                           refine_margin=0.01, force_refine=False, adaptive_alpha=False):\n    \"\"\"True oracle SDF refinement using GT SDF + numerical gradient.\"\"\"\n    assert sdf_gt_data is not None\n    current_ee = obs.get(\"robot0_eef_pos\", None)\n    assert current_ee is not None, \"robot0_eef_pos not in obs\"\n    next_ee = current_ee + action[:3]\n    sdf_value = query_gt_sdf(next_ee, sdf_gt_data)\n    info = {\"sdf_value\": sdf_value, \"refined\": False, \"gradient\": None}\n    if force_refine or sdf_value < refine_margin:\n        grad = np.zeros(3)\n        for i in range(3):\n            pos_plus = next_ee.copy()\n            pos_plus[i] += delta\n            pos_minus = next_ee.copy()\n            pos_minus[i] -= delta\n            grad[i] = (query_gt_sdf(pos_plus, sdf_gt_data) - query_gt_sdf(pos_minus, sdf_gt_data)) / (2 * delta)\n        grad_norm = np.linalg.norm(grad)\n        if grad_norm > 1e-8:\n            grad_dir = grad / grad_norm\n            action_refined = action.copy()\n            action_norm = np.linalg.norm(action[:3])\n            if adaptive_alpha and action_norm > 1e-6:\n                alpha_use = min(alpha, 0.5 * action_norm)\n            else:\n                alpha_use = alpha if action_norm > 1e-6 else 0.0\n            action_refined[:3] = action[:3] + alpha_use * grad_dir\n            info[\"refined\"] = True\n            info[\"gradient\"] = grad.tolist()\n            info[\"gradient_magnitude\"] = float(grad_norm)\n            info[\"alpha_used\"] = float(alpha_use)\n            info[\"action_norm\"] = float(action_norm)\n            return action_refined, info\n    return action, info\n\n\n'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(\n    '\\ndef evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, task_id):',\n    oracle_funcs + 'def evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, task_id, sdf_gt_data=None):',\n    1\n)\n\n# 4. Add tracking variables\ncontent = content.replace(\n    '        action_norms = []\\n        action_vectors = []\\n',\n    '        action_norms = []\\n        action_vectors = []\\n        ep_refine_count = 0\\n        ep_grad_magnitudes = []\\n        ep_sdf_values = []\\n',\n    1\n)\n\n# 5. Insert oracle refinement after action = action_plan.popleft()\nold_action = '            action = action_plan.popleft()\\n            action_arr = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\\n'\nnew_action = \"\"\"            action = action_plan.popleft()\n\n            # Action-space oracle SDF refinement\n            if args.true_oracle and sdf_gt_data is not None:\n                action = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\n                action, refine_info = _refine_action_oracle(\n                    action, obs, sdf_gt_data,\n                    alpha=args.refine_alpha, refine_margin=args.refine_margin,\n                    force_refine=args.force_refine_oracle,\n                    adaptive_alpha=args.adaptive_alpha,\n                )\n                if refine_info.get(\"sdf_value\") is not None:\n                    ep_sdf_values.append(refine_info[\"sdf_value\"])\n                if refine_info.get(\"refined\"):\n                    ep_refine_count += 1\n                if refine_info.get(\"gradient_magnitude\"):\n                    ep_grad_magnitudes.append(refine_info[\"gradient_magnitude\"])\n\n            action_arr = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\n\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_action, new_action, 1)\n\n# 6. Add refine stats to episode result\nold_result_end = \"\"\"            \"time_s\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        }\n        results.append(result)\"\"\"\nnew_result_end = \"\"\"            \"time_s\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        }\n        if args.true_oracle:\n            result[\"refine_count\"] = ep_refine_count\n            if ep_grad_magnitudes:\n                result[\"gradient_magnitude_avg\"] = float(np.mean(ep_grad_magnitudes))\n            if ep_sdf_values:\n                result[\"sdf_mean\"] = float(np.mean(ep_sdf_values))\n                result[\"sdf_min\"] = float(np.min(ep_sdf_values))\n        results.append(result)\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_result_end, new_result_end, 1)\n\n# 7. Add refine info to per-episode logging\nold_log = \"\"\"        frozen_str = \"FROZEN\" if is_frozen else \"\"\n        logging.info(\n            \"  ep %d/%d: %s | %s %s| ee_disp=%.4f | min_obs=%.4f | steps=%d | %.1fs\",\n            ep + 1, args.n_episodes, status, coll_str, frozen_str,\n            robot_total_displacement, result[\"min_ee_obstacle_distance\"],\n            step + 1, elapsed,\n        )\"\"\"\nnew_log = \"\"\"        frozen_str = \"FROZEN\" if is_frozen else \"\"\n        refine_str = f\" refined={ep_refine_count}\" if args.true_oracle else \"\"\n        logging.info(\n            \"  ep %d/%d: %s | %s %s| ee_disp=%.4f | min_obs=%.4f | steps=%d | %.1fs%s\",\n            ep + 1, args.n_episodes, status, coll_str, frozen_str,\n            robot_total_displacement, result[\"min_ee_obstacle_distance\"],\n            step + 1, elapsed, refine_str,\n        )\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_log, new_log, 1)\n\n# 8. Add refinement stats to task summary\nold_summary_end = \"\"\"        \"episodes\": results,\n    }\n\n    logging.info(\"Task %d: TSR=%.1f%% CAR=%.1f%% frozen=%.1f%% real_safe_cand=%d\",\"\"\"\nnew_summary_end = \"\"\"        \"episodes\": results,\n    }\n    if args.true_oracle:\n        total_refines = sum(r.get(\"refine_count\", 0) for r in results)\n        summary[\"total_refine_count\"] = total_refines\n        summary[\"avg_refine_per_episode\"] = float(total_refines / len(results)) if results else 0.0\n        all_grads = [r[\"gradient_magnitude_avg\"] for r in results if \"gradient_magnitude_avg\" in r]\n        if all_grads:\n            summary[\"avg_gradient_magnitude\"] = float(np.mean(all_grads))\n\n    logging.info(\"Task %d: TSR=%.1f%% CAR=%.1f%% frozen=%.1f%% real_safe_cand=%d\",\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_summary_end, new_summary_end, 1)\n\n# 9. Add oracle args to parse_args\nold_parse_end = \"\"\"    parser.add_argument(\"--baseline_frozen_rate\", type=float, default=None,\n                        help=\"Baseline frozen rate for pass/fail gate (e.g. 0.05 for 5%%)\")\n    return parser.parse_args()\"\"\"\nnew_parse_end = \"\"\"    parser.add_argument(\"--baseline_frozen_rate\", type=float, default=None,\n                        help=\"Baseline frozen rate for pass/fail gate (e.g. 0.05 for 5%%)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--true_oracle\", action=\"store_true\", default=False,\n                        help=\"Use GT SDF data for true oracle refinement\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--sdf_gt_path\", type=str, default=None,\n                        help=\"Path to GT SDF npz file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--refine_alpha\", type=float, default=0.01,\n                        help=\"Oracle refinement step size\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--refine_margin\", type=float, default=0.05,\n                        help=\"SDF threshold below which refinement activates\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--adaptive_alpha\", action=\"store_true\", default=False,\n                        help=\"Use adaptive alpha: min(refine_alpha, 0.5 * action_norm)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--force_refine_oracle\", action=\"store_true\", default=False,\n                        help=\"Force oracle refinement every step\")\n    return parser.parse_args()\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_parse_end, new_parse_end, 1)\n\n# 10. Load SDF GT in main()\nold_main_eval = \"\"\"    logging.info(\"Tasks to evaluate: %s (%d episodes each)\", task_ids, args.n_episodes)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for tid in task_ids:\n        result = evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, tid)\"\"\"\nnew_main_eval = \"\"\"    logging.info(\"Tasks to evaluate: %s (%d episodes each)\", task_ids, args.n_episodes)\n\n    # Load GT SDF for oracle refinement\n    sdf_gt_data = None\n    if args.true_oracle:\n        assert args.sdf_gt_path, \"--true_oracle requires --sdf_gt_path\"\n        assert os.path.exists(args.sdf_gt_path), f\"GT SDF not found: {args.sdf_gt_path}\"\n        sdf_gt_data = _load_sdf_gt_oracle(args.sdf_gt_path)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for tid in task_ids:\n        result = evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, tid, sdf_gt_data=sdf_gt_data)\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_main_eval, new_main_eval, 1)\n\n# 11. Add oracle config to output JSON\nold_output_end = \"\"\"        \"per_task\": all_results,\n    }\n\n    with open(save_path, \"w\") as f:\"\"\"\nnew_output_end = \"\"\"        \"per_task\": all_results,\n    }\n    if args.true_oracle:\n        output[\"config\"][\"true_oracle\"] = True\n        output[\"config\"][\"force_refine_oracle\"] = args.force_refine_oracle\n        output[\"config\"][\"adaptive_alpha\"] = args.adaptive_alpha\n        output[\"config\"][\"refine_alpha\"] = args.refine_alpha\n        output[\"config\"][\"refine_margin\"] = args.refine_margin\n        output[\"config\"][\"sdf_gt_path\"] = args.sdf_gt_path\n        total_refines = sum(r.get(\"total_refine_count\", 0) for r in all_results)\n        output[\"overall\"][\"total_refine_count\"] = total_refines\n        output[\"overall\"][\"avg_refine_per_episode\"] = float(total_refines / total_episodes) if total_episodes else 0.0\n\n    with open(save_path, \"w\") as f:\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_output_end, new_output_end, 1)\n\nif content == original:\n    print(\"ERROR: No changes were made!\")\n    sys.exit(1)\n\nwith open(filepath, 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\n\nprint(\"PATCH OK - all modifications applied\")\nPATCHEOF\npython3 /tmp/patch_pi05.py","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":11582,"cache_read_input_tokens":89389,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":11582},"output_tokens":64,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f5d25fdc-d6a6-49b4-a25f-0fae41cc1f92","uuid":"ad1a1fe6-7712-4997-a85a-c9302bd5eaa1"}

Add oracle functions before evaluate_task + modify signature\noracle_funcs = '''\n\n# --- Oracle SDF refinement (ported from eval_safelibero.py) ---\n\ndef _load_sdf_gt_oracle(path):\n    \"\"\"Load GT SDF data for true oracle refinement.\"\"\"\n    data = np.load(path)\n    points = data[\"query_points\"].astype(np.float64)\n    sdf_values = data[\"sdf_values\"].astype(np.float64)\n    tree = cKDTree(points)\n    logging.info(\"Loaded GT SDF oracle: %d points from %s\", len(points), path)\n    return {\"points\": points, \"sdf_values\": sdf_values, \"tree\": tree}\n\n\ndef query_gt_sdf(position, sdf_gt_data, k=8):\n    \"\"\"Query GT SDF at position using IDW interpolation.\"\"\"\n    dists, idxs = sdf_gt_data[\"tree\"].query(position, k=k)\n    if k == 1:\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs])\n    if dists[0] < 1e-10:\n        return float(sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs[0]])\n    weights = 1.0 / dists\n    weights /= weights.sum()\n    return float(np.dot(weights, sdf_gt_data[\"sdf_values\"][idxs]))\n\n\ndef _refine_action_oracle(action, obs, sdf_gt_data, alpha=0.1, delta=0.001,\n                           refine_margin=0.01, force_refine=False, adaptive_alpha=False):\n    \"\"\"True oracle SDF refinement using GT SDF + numerical gradient.\"\"\"\n    assert sdf_gt_data is not None\n    current_ee = obs.get(\"robot0_eef_pos\", None)\n    assert current_ee is not None, \"robot0_eef_pos not in obs\"\n    next_ee = current_ee + action[:3]\n    sdf_value = query_gt_sdf(next_ee, sdf_gt_data)\n    info = {\"sdf_value\": sdf_value, \"refined\": False, \"gradient\": None}\n    if force_refine or sdf_value < refine_margin:\n        grad = np.zeros(3)\n        for i in range(3):\n            pos_plus = next_ee.copy()\n            pos_plus[i] += delta\n            pos_minus = next_ee.copy()\n            pos_minus[i] -= delta\n            grad[i] = (query_gt_sdf(pos_plus, sdf_gt_data) - query_gt_sdf(pos_minus, sdf_gt_data)) / (2 * delta)\n        grad_norm = np.linalg.norm(grad)\n        if grad_norm > 1e-8:\n            grad_dir = grad / grad_norm\n            action_refined = action.copy()\n            action_norm = np.linalg.norm(action[:3])\n            if adaptive_alpha and action_norm > 1e-6:\n                alpha_use = min(alpha, 0.5 * action_norm)\n            else:\n                alpha_use = alpha if action_norm > 1e-6 else 0.0\n            action_refined[:3] = action[:3] + alpha_use * grad_dir\n            info[\"refined\"] = True\n            info[\"gradient\"] = grad.tolist()\n            info[\"gradient_magnitude\"] = float(grad_norm)\n            info[\"alpha_used\"] = float(alpha_use)\n            info[\"action_norm\"] = float(action_norm)\n            return action_refined, info\n    return action, info\n\n\n'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(\n    '\\ndef evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, task_id):',\n    oracle_funcs + 'def evaluate_task(args, client, benchmark, task_id, sdf_gt_data=None):',\n    1\n)\n\n# 4. Add tracking variables\ncontent = content.replace(\n    '        action_norms = []\\n        action_vectors = []\\n',\n    '        action_norms = []\\n        action_vectors = []\\n        ep_refine_count = 0\\n        ep_grad_magnitudes = []\\n        ep_sdf_values = []\\n',\n    1\n)\n\n# 5. Insert oracle refinement after action = action_plan.popleft()\nold_action = '            action = action_plan.popleft()\\n            action_arr = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\\n'\nnew_action = \"\"\"            action = action_plan.popleft()\n\n            # Action-space oracle SDF refinement\n            if args.true_oracle and sdf_gt_data is not None:\n                action = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\n                action, refine_info = _refine_action_oracle(\n                    action, obs, sdf_gt_data,\n                    alpha=args.refine_alpha, refine_margin=args.refine_margin,\n                    force_refine=args.force_refine_oracle,\n                    adaptive_alpha=args.adaptive_alpha,\n                )\n                if refine_info.get(\"sdf_value\") is not None:\n                    ep_sdf_values.append(refine_info[\"sdf_value\"])\n                if refine_info.get(\"refined\"):\n                    ep_refine_count += 1\n                if refine_info.get(\"gradient_magnitude\"):\n                    ep_grad_magnitudes.append(refine_info[\"gradient_magnitude\"])\n\n            action_arr = np.asarray(action, dtype=np.float64)\n\"\"\"\ncontent = content.replace(old_action, new_action, 1)\n\n# 6. Add refinement stats to task summary\nold_summary_end = '''        \"episodes\": results,\n    }\n\n    logging.info(\"Task %d: TSR=%.1f%% CAR=%.1f%% frozen=%.1f%% real_safe_cand=%d\",'''\nnew_summary_end = '''        \"episodes\": results,\n    }\n    if args.true_oracle:\n        total_refines = sum(r.get(\"refine_count\", 0) for r in results)\n        summary[\"total_refine_count\"] = total_refines\n        summary[\"avg_refine_per_episode\"] = float(total_refines / len(results)) if results else 0.0\n        all_grads = [r[\"gradient_magnitude_avg\"] for r in results if \"gradient_magnitude_avg\" in r]\n        if all_grads:\n            summary[\"avg_gradient_magnitude\"] = float(np.mean(all_grads))\n\n    logging.info(\"Task %d: TSR=%.1f%% CAR=%.1f%% frozen=%.1f%% real_safe_cand=%d\",'''\ncontent = content.replace(old_summary_end, new_summary_end, 1)\n\n# 9.
